# RECUT: `cashflow` (Cash Flow & NPV) and `fiscal` (Fiscal Regime Design) onto the PIA 2021 compliance repair

Phase 1 report, 2026-09-26. NextGen branch `fix/cashflow-fiscal-pia-recut` (worktree `/root/wt-ec7-recut`, off
origin/main 08f17b61d). Engines: petrolord-engines 3778451 (#262, engines 3.12.0). Lead decision applied: the courses
teach the CORRECTED engine (default path); no lesson frames the repair as a story; the three stated readings
(price-royalty base year Act 2020 vs Regulations 2021; the NTA deep offshore HCT reading; the new-acreage PML HCT rate
15 vs 30) are taught as open questions and never graded. Nothing merged, no PR, no production contact.

## 1. Vendoring (commit e05ea2f1e)

- 16 paths copied sha-identical from 3778451, each checked by `git hash-object` against the canonical blob:
  `engines/economics/{cashflow.ts,fiscalRegime.js,fiscalTemplates.js}`, `__tests__/economics.{cashflow.test.ts,
  fiscal.test.js,pia2021.test.ts}`, `test-data/economics/goldens/{cashflow,fiscal,pia2021}_cases.json`,
  `test-data/economics/fixtures/pia-worked-example.json`, `tools/validation/economics/{oracle_cashflow.py,oracle_fiscal.py,
  oracle_pia2021.py,negcontrol_pia2021.sh,FINDINGS-pia2021.md,AUDIT-PIA-2021.md}`. `fiscalConventions.js` and
  `irrContract.js` already matched. Pin stays at 54cf7c4 (repo convention since D1-D5); the 16 paths are ledgered in
  `packages/engines/VENDOR.json` as group `ec7-pia-recut` at their 3778451 blobs.
- The HELD montecarlo golden (group 6 deviation) takes engines #262's four-line change (`"pia_legacy_pre_audit": true` on
  its four PIA configs) as a backport onto the held blob; its ledger pin moves with it. Without it the held montecarlo suite
  goes red (21 tests) against the new cashflow.ts.
- `check-vendored-engines --canonical /root/petrolord-engines`: clean, 313 recorded deviations.
- `run-vendored-engine-tests`: 165 suites, 15,553 tests, 24 failing = exactly the 24 allow-listed fdp/screening rows;
  pia2021 603/603.

inputs printed; refusals printed verbatim, and the three refused published cases (`allowance_cap_midyear`,

printed stated input. Sections 14-16 and 20 rewritten (weighted tranches, gas 5%/2.5%, benchmarks on both bases, HCT rate
table, 4 USD after-cap allowance, five-year capital allowance, NDDC on total budget, framework per year, one ledger across
2025-2026); new Section 22 (texts with read date, `pia_notes`, the engine's pia2021 Ekene cases) and Section 23 (the three

fiscal: the sliding-royalty and R-factor sections now teach on a labelled "tiered teaching regime" (the old template
numbers, no country's values); new subsections print the re-based PIA template's `pia_2021` royalty, liquids-gross cost
limit and cumulative-production split on ODIDI/the default project; Section 1 instrument list, the payback-tie count
(computed now) and the `$1339.3MM` example corrected. `ec2_fields.mjs` now spells `cmp_psc_capex_loss_last_tenth_musd`
(the waves.json `fieldsNoGeneratorSpells` exception is retired). fiscalRegime.js imports cashflow.ts, so the kit carries

L677); fiscal L1310 "every regime recovers cost at its own limit" is true but now on two different bases.

## 5. Decisions needed from the lead

- **L1. Expert capstone.** The live IKPOTO Expert prompt cannot run on the corrected engine. Options: (a) accept the
  proposed set above (four new keys, a new graded-field migration, capstone prompt rewrite, attempts guard); (b) keep the
  six keys and state the new-PML rate in the prompt, which grades `pia_total_hct_usd` and `pia_npv_real_usd` on a stated
  input the rule calls an open question; (c) make IKPOTO a CONVERTED lease (HCT 30% settled) and re-pick fields (the
  cap-crossing lesson then moves to a converted-lease allowance, no cap). Recommendation: (a) or (c).
- **L2. Price royalty base year in graded fields.** Every NTA-era case with a price above the low benchmark carries the
  price royalty, which differs between the Regulations 2021 base (engine default) and the Act's 2020 base; any graded
  royalty, HCT, CIT, levy or NPV then depends on it (on IKPOTO: price royalty, CIT and levy do). COURSE-LINE.md section 3
  says "the course keys only the Regulations' reading and says so". Confirm that reading (stated in the prompt) is
  acceptable for cashflow/fiscal, or require capstone prices at or below the low benchmark (then the price royalty field
  must go).
- **L3.** `pia_2034_cpr_deferred_usd` is moved only by ignoring the working interest; accept it, or swap for another field.
- **L4. Legacy lab replays.** FINDINGS says cashflowLab.js may switch to `PIA_LEGACY_PRE_AUDIT`; under the lead decision
  the lab is re-cut to the default path (golden replays drop the flag). Confirm.
- **L5. Pre-existing repair-history wording** ("since engines 3.10.0", JV/IRR/profile-point stories) in cashflow
  lessons (not logged by the lessons audit) and in 25 cashflow bank rows (logged as framing). The no-history rule reads as
  covering them; confirm they are in scope for phase 2.
- **L6. fiscal "Nigerian PIA (PSC)".** The Designer's default regime (Suite sample values, not the Act) carries a
  Nigerian name in the golden and in lessons/banks. Keep the name with a sentence that its values are the Designer's
  samples (current digest wording), or ask the engines/Suite side to rename it.
- **L7.** cashflow Expert final ord 25: the banks audit flagged a key about abandonment cost scaling at WI below 100
  that is wrong independently of this recut (the older EC1-3 change: the entered amount is the share under both modes). Included as graded; fix in
  the same pass unless the lead objects.
